# ATLAS refresh: per-entry field-level delta

ATLAS pin 2026.09 -> 2026.10. Before = `origin/main:data/incidents.json (6fa029ca)`, after = `data/incidents.json on branch ws4/atlas-2026-10-refresh-2026-10-29 after rebuild`. Generated by `scripts/atlas_entry_delta.py`; do not hand-edit. (DO-NOT-REGENERATE once committed: it is a dated record of one refresh.)

## Declared context (written by the pipeline engineer, 2026-10-29)

**What changed and why.** The pinned ATLAS release moved 2026.09 -> 2026.10 (`ingest/atlas/ATLAS-2026.10.yaml`, sha256 `ac41ee9f99a47084ba5fdfc77ae83a28687e12390240fffeb37df7914ffe142d`, chain `ATLAS-latest.yaml` -> `v6/ATLAS-latest.yaml` -> `ATLAS-2026.10.yaml`). Counts below are ENTRIES (not occurrences). 1,744 entries change `mitre_atlas` and/or `mitre_atlas_tactics`; no entry added, removed, tombstoned or re-IDed; the other 14,040 are canonical-JSON identical.

1. **Superseded technique ids (mechanical translation, `mappings/atlas_id_translations.json`, `since: 2026.10`).** Four moves are stated by the upstream v2026.10 release notes ("moved from ..."): `AML.T0024.000` -> `AML.T0137.000` (0 entries), `AML.T0024.001` -> `AML.T0137.002` (3), `AML.T0056` -> `AML.T0085.003` (733), `AML.T0057` -> `AML.T0085.002` (1,082). **`AML.T0024.002` ("Extract AI Model", 4 entries: INC-04855, INC-06373, INC-07224, INC-05595) has no successor in the release notes**; per ruling D149 it is mapped to its still-defined parent `AML.T0024` ("Exfiltration via AI Inference API", tactic `AML.TA0010`, defined in the 2026.10 YAML) as a conservative generalisation (the `AML.T0015.001` -> `AML.T0015` precedent). This is a loss of specificity, not a semantic re-target; that review is pending with label-scientist. `scripts/scrape_aiid.py` still emits `AML.T0024.002`/`.000`; the translation applies at merge (`fill_taxonomy`) so fresh rows are covered.
2. **Tactic moves.** `AML.T0056`/`AML.T0057` carried `AML.TA0010` (Exfiltration); their targets `AML.T0085.003`/`.002` achieve `AML.TA0009` (Collection) via their parent. Entries therefore gain `AML.TA0009` and lose `AML.TA0010` only when no remaining technique achieves it (the pattern table shows both cases). `AML.T0005` gained `AML.TA0003` in the release, but **0 entries carry `AML.T0005` or its subtechniques**, so that link change moves no entry.
3. **One mechanism not previously covered.** A curation override (D130-2L, the AIjacking row `INC-02899`) pins `mitre_atlas` as a literal list that included `AML.T0057`; overrides apply after translation, so the id would have survived as deprecated and failed `lint_atlas_ids`. `apply_curation_overrides` now passes an override-set `mitre_atlas` through the same translation (scripts change, no override file edit). `INC-02899` is the entry in the pattern table with a technique change and no tactic change (its override already listed `AML.TA0009` and `AML.TA0010`).

**Other fields that move, by design.** `updated` is stamped with the build date (2026-10-29) and `last_seen` follows it; these moved on exactly the 1,744 changed-code entries and no others (the tool compares every field of every entry; 0 untouched-entry violations). Top-level `generated` and `stats.json` `taxonomy_versions.atlas` move. Regenerated artifacts: `data/incidents.min.json`, `docs/data/incidents.min.json`, `src/genai_incidents/data/incidents.min.json` (+ `taxonomy_versions.json`), `INCIDENTS.md`, `docs/incidents/*.md` shards, `README.md` (`built` date).

**Scope notes.** 396 of the changed entries have an `OECD-*` source id and 144 an `AIID-*` one (mixed-source rows). Only their ATLAS code fields and `updated`/`last_seen` moved; no OECD or AIID input file was touched and no description, severity, category or OECD-derived content field changed (D114: no lift). 5 changed entries are `retracted` (3) or `unverified` (2) and are not exported to STIX.

**Independent check (agreement 6).** Not the tool's method: a separate script reads origin/main's `incidents.json`, this branch's, and the RAW upstream YAML `achieves` relationships (not `mappings/mitre_atlas.json`), and verified per entry: after technique set == translation of before set (hard-coded 5-id table taken from the release notes + D149); every tactic derivable from the after techniques is present; every tactic added is derivable; every tactic dropped is `AML.TA0010` and not derivable from any remaining technique. Result: 1,744 changed, 0 violations. **Proved to fire:** pointing its `AML.T0057` target at `AML.T0085.003` made it report 1,082 technique-set failures.

**Tool change in this refresh.** `scripts/atlas_entry_delta.py` previously labelled `AML.TA0009` "UNEXPLAINED" when it arrived through a translation target's own links (1,739 false UNEXPLAINED on the first run); it now attributes such a tactic to the translation target. Every changed entry below has a stated reason; UNEXPLAINED count 0.
